periyōrkaḷē, maṉitaṉ vāḻap piṟantavaṉ. avaṉ vāḻattāṉ vēṇṭum. piṟarai mōcam ceytā? aracāṅkattai ēmāṟṟiyā? aṉṟu, aṉṟu. uṭal varunta uḻaittup poruḷīṭṭi vāḻavēṇṭum. ovvoruvarum tam kuṭumpattil kuṟainta aḷavu tēvaikkut takkatait tam uḻaippāl peṟattakka muṟaiyil toḻil muṟaiyum kūli muṟaiyum amaiyavēṇṭum. ellā makkaḷum paṭṭiṉiyiṉṟi uṇpārāyiṉ, nāṭṭil kolai, tiruṭu, koḷḷai mutaliyaṉa pēraḷavu kuṟaintu viṭum. makkaḷ vāḻvataṟku vaḻi ceyyāmal avarkaḷait tīcceyalil pukumpaṭi ceytuviṭṭu, aracāṅkam ciṟaiccālaikaḷai maṭṭum kaṭṭuvatil poruḷumillai; payaṉumillai. `nāṭellām vāḻak kēṭoṉṟumillai’ eṉṟa paḻamoḻiyiṉ poruḷai nām uṇaravēṇṭum.
kōvilukkuc celvatum, tēṅkāy paḻam uṭaippatum, camayakkuṟikaḷai neṟṟiyil iṭuvatumtāṉ iṟaippaṟṟu eṉṟu cilar eṇṇukiṉṟaṉar. itu tavaṟu. iṟaivaṉāl paṭaikkap paṭṭa ellā uyirkaḷaiyum taṉṉaippōl eṇṇac ceyvatu iṟaippaṟṟu; ellōriṭattilum aṉpōṭum aruḷōṭum naṭakkacceyvatu iṟaippaṟṟu; taṉṉiṭam vāṅkupavarai ēmāṟṟāta vaṇikaṉ iṟaippaṟṟu uṭaiyavaṉ; taṉ ālaiyil vēlai ceyyum toḻilāḷarkku vāḻvaḷikkum mutalāḷi iṟaippaṟṟu uṭaiyavaṉ; ivvāṟu taṉ kaṭamaiyaic ceyyum kāvalaṉ, aluvalāḷaṉ mutaliya aṉaivarumē iṟaippaṟṟu uṭaiyavar. camutāya nalaṉukku uḻaikkum ovvōr uṇmait toṇṭaṉum iṟaippaṟṟu uṭaiyavaṉāvāṉ.
neṟṟiyil tirunīṟu pūcikkoṇṭu paṭiyiṉ aḷavaik kuṟaittup potu makkaḷukku viṟpavaṉum, aintu kecat tuṇiyai nālaraik kecamāka aḷappavaṉum, paṭṭai nāmattaip pōṭṭukkoṇṭu miḷakāyttūḷil cemmaṇ poṭiyaik kalantu viṟpavaṉum, makkaḷukkup puriyātavaṟṟaic collic camayattiṉ peyarālum teyvaṅkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷālum avarkaḷai ēmāṟṟip piḻaippavarum iṟaippaṟṟu uṭaiyavar allar. camayac coṟpoḻivukaḷ ceytukoṇṭē kaimpeṇkaḷai vēṭṭaiyāṭum kāmukarum, kaṭṭiya maṉaivi irukkak kaṇṭōrai nāṭum kayavarum nāḷtōṟum kōvilukkuc ceṉṟālum, kōvilil nivantaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭuttiṉālum, tāṉa tarumaṅkaḷ ceytālum, iṟaippaṟṟu uṭaiyavar ākār. ivarai iṟaippaṟṟu uṭaiyavar eṉṟu ivartam kūlikaḷum, veḷivēṭattaik kaṇṭu mayaṅkum pētaiyarum kūṟalām; āṉāl aṟivuṭaiyavar kūṟār.
perumakkaḷē, taṉ maṉaccāṭcikku matippukkoṭuttu aṟavaḻiyil naṭappavaṉē iṟaippaṟṟu uṭaiyavaṉ. avaṉ tiruvaṭikaḷ eṉ muṭimēl taṅkuka. ap perumakaṉukkeṉṟu oru camayam illai. oru cāti illai; avaṉ ovvoru camayattilumuḷḷa nallavaṟṟai eṭuttukkoḷvāṉ; avaṟṟaip pārāṭṭuvāṉ; taṉ vāḻkkaiyil kaṭaippiṭippāṉ. avaṉiṭam camaya veṟiyaik kāṇa muṭiyātu. avaṉ iṉpattilum tuṉpattilum muka malarcciyōṭu iruppāṉ.
periyōrkaḷē, eṉ pēccai muṭikkumuṉ uṅkaḷukku oru vēṇṭukōḷ viṭukkiṟēṉ. uṅkaḷ āṇ piḷḷaikaḷaip pōlavē peṇ piḷḷaikaḷaiyum paṭikka vaiyuṅkaḷ. ovvoru peṇṇum taṉṉaḷavil campātikkum takuti peṟavēṇṭum. peṇtāṉ camutāyattiṉ uyir nāṭi. avaḷ aṟivum, taṉṉaik kāttukkoḷḷum takutiyum peṟṟiruntāl tāṉ avaḷai maṇantukoḷpavaṉ avaḷukku matippait taruvāṉ. avaḷum avvappōtu taṉ kaṇavaṉ kuṟaikaḷaic cuṭṭikkāṭṭit tirutta muṭiyum. nam piḷḷaikaḷukkuc celvam tēvaiyillai; kalviyum aṟivum maṉattiṇmaiyumē tēvai yāṉaiva. avarkaḷ avaṟṟaip peṟumpaṭi ceyvatē peṟṟōr kaṭamaiyākum. attakaiya piḷḷaikaḷāltāṉ namatu camutāyam etirkālattil cīrum ciṟappum peṟum.”
maṇivaṇṇaṉatu iccoṟpoḻivu potumakkaḷ uḷḷaṅkaḷil āḻamākap patintatu. avarkaḷ kaikaḷai neṭunēram taṭṭit taṅkaḷ makiḻcciyait terivittuk koṇṭaṉar. maṅkai maṇivaṇṇaṉatu maṇi pōṉṟa coṟkaḷaic ceviyārap parukiṉāḷ. kaṭainteṭutta uṇmaikaḷai avaṉ kūṟak kūṟa, avaḷ mukamalarcci koṇṭu talai acaittāḷ. muṉpē avaṉatu kaṭṭaḻakilum paṇivāṉa pēccilum īṭupāṭukoṇṭa avaḷ ippoḻutu taṉ uḷḷattaiyē avaṉiṭam paṟikoṭuttāḷ.
aḻakāpuri aṭikaḷ aḷavaṟṟa makiḻcciyōṭu maṇivaṇṇaṉaip palapaṭap pārāṭṭik kūṭṭattai muṭittār. muttappar mukamalarcciyōṭu maṇivaṇṇaṉ kaikaḷaip paṟṟik kulukkiṉār. nakkīraṉ makiḻccip perukkil maṇivaṇṇaṉait taḻuvikkoṇṭāṉ.
aṉṟu iravu ellōrum aṅku uṇavu uṇṭaṉar. maṅkai taṉ tantaiyait taṉiyē aḻaittu, “appā, nāṉ virumpumpōtu tirumaṇam ceytukoḷvēṉ eṉṟu kūṟiyatu uṅkaḷukku niṉaivirukkiṟatā?” eṉṟu cirittukkoṇṭē kēṭṭāḷ. maṇivaṇṇaṉaik kaṇṭanāḷ mutal maṅkaiyiṭam kāṇappaṭṭa māṟṟattaik kaṇṭu makiḻcci aṭaintirunta muttappar mella nakaittu, “ām ammā, naṉku niṉaivirukkiṟatu” eṉṟār.
“appā, nāṉ maṇivaṇṇaṉait tirumaṇam ceytu koḷḷa virumpukiṟēṉ. avarum eṉ karuttai makiḻcciyōṭu varavēṟpār eṉṟu nampukiṟēṉ. avaraip pakkuvamākak kēṭṭup pāruṅkaḷ” eṉṟu maṅkai ciṟitu nāṇattuṭaṉ kūṟiṉāḷ.
muttappar ellaiyaṟṟa makiḻcci aṭaintār. avar maṇivaṇṇaṉait taṉiyē aḻaittu atupaṟṟip pēcumpaṭi aḻakāpuri aṭikaḷiṭam kūṟiṉār. aṭikaḷ aṭainta makiḻccikku ellai illai. avar kūṟiyataik kēṭṭa maṇivaṇṇaṉ, “aṭikaḷē, eṉ maṉam entap peṇṇaik kaṇṭum kalakkam koṇṭatillai; eṉ vacam iḻantatillai. āṉāl maṅkaiyaik kaṇṭa nāḷ mutal eṉ maṉattai eṉṉāl kaṭṭuppaṭutta muṭiyavillai. nāṉ anta ammaiyai uḷamāra virumpukiṟēṉ. avarōṭu iruntu, aṟaneṟiyil naṭantu, toṇṭu ceyyak kāttirukkiṟēṉ” eṉṟu ārvattōṭu kūṟiṉāṉ.

tiruppōrūrp paṇṇaiyār makaḷ- maṅkaiyar māṇikkam-maṅkaiyarkaracikkum pulavar maṇivaṇṇaṉ em.ē.kkum aḻakāpuri aṭikaḷ talaimaiyil tirumaṇam eṉṟa ceyti nāṭu muḻutum paraviyatu. aḻaippaip peṟṟukkoṇṭa amaiccarkaḷum, uyar aluvalarum, pala ūrc celvarum, potu makkaḷum tiraḷākak kuḻumiyiruntaṉar. kōṭīcuvariyāṉa maṅkaiyarkakaraci eṉṟum aṇiyum iṉṟiyamaiyāta aṇikaḷōṭu eḷiya uṭaiyil maṇappeṇṇākak kāṭciyaḷittāḷ. avaḷatu maṉap paṇpaik kaṇṭu avaiyōr pārāṭṭiṉar. tēvāra muḻakkattiṟkiṭaiyē- pal iyaṅkaḷ olikka- periyōr vāḻttuk kūṟat tirumaṇam iṉitu naṭaipeṟṟatu. maṅkaiyarkkaraci taṉ irupatteṭṭām vayatil maṇivaṇṇaṉukku uriyavaḷ āṉāḷ.
34. amaiccar patavi
ēḻāṇṭukaḷukku muṉ toṭaṅkappeṟṟa marakatavalli aṟanilaiyattiṉ uyarnilaip paḷḷi ēḻām āṇṭiṉ toṭakkattil oru kallūriyāka vaḷarcci aṭaintatu; anātai viṭuti munnūṟu pērkaḷaik koṇṭatāka viḷaṅkiyatu; toḻiṟkūṭam palvēṟu kuṭicaik kaittoḻil kaḷukku iṭam aḷittu virivaṭaintatu; aṅku taccut toḻil, tōlaik koṇṭu poruḷkaḷaic ceyyum toḻil mutaliyaṉa vaḷarcciyaṭaintaṉa.
karumpākkattil toṭaṅkappeṟṟa vaḷḷiyammai aṟanilaiyam (pāṇṭiyaṉ tāyār peyar) paḷḷi, anātai viṭuti, toḻiṟkūṭam ākiya mūṉṟaiyum peṟṟu vaḷarat toṭaṅkiyatu. ceṅkaṟpaṭṭukku aṇmaiyiluḷḷa kuḷattūr paṇṇaiyil tōṟṟuvikkap peṟṟa kōmaḷavalli aṟanilaiyamum (kaṇṇaṉ tāyār peyar) vaḷarpiṟai pōla vaḷaralāyiṟṟu. aḻakāpuri aṭikaḷ tām irunta ciṟṟūril irunta celvarkaḷiṉ utaviyāl maṇimēkalai aṟanilaiyattait toṭaṅkivaittār; aṅku anātai viṭutiyum, naṭunilaip paḷḷiyum, toḻiṟ kūṭamum ciṟanta muṟaiyil vēlai ceytu varalāyiṉa.
muttappar, maṇivaṇṇaṉ, maṅkai, aṭikaḷ ākiya nālvarum maṅkaiyiṉ tirumaṇattiṟkup piṉpu ceṅkaṟpaṭṭu māvaṭṭam muḻumaiyum pirayāṇam ceytaṉar. ovvoru periya ūrilum irunta celvarkaḷiṉ utaviyāl anātai viṭuti, paḷḷi, toḻiṟkūṭam ākiya mūṉṟaiyum ēṟpaṭuttum paṭi vaṟpuṟuttiṉar; kirāmantōṟum ceṉṟu, ammakkaḷukkut tēvaiyāṉa nalluraikaḷai vaḻaṅkiṉar. iṅṅaṉam avarkaḷatu iṭaiviṭā uḻaippiṉāl ōrāṇṭiṟkuḷ am māvaṭṭattil cila periya ūrkaḷil aṟa nilaiyaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭaṉa.
avarkaḷatu ceyaṟkariya ceyalai aṭikkaṭi ceytittāḷkaḷiṉ vāyilāka aṟinta piṟa ūrkaḷil irunta perumakkaḷ tattam ūrkaḷukku varumpaṭi avarkaḷukku aḻaippu viṭuttaṉar. avarkaḷ avvaḻaippiṉpaṭi ovvoru māvaṭṭattiṟkum ceṉṟaṉar; āṅkāṅku tam tiṭṭattai viḷakkip pēciṉar. maṇivaṇṇaṉ, maṅkai ākiya iruvaruṭaiya coṟpoḻivukaḷ potumakkaḷiṉ uḷḷaṅkaḷaik kavarntaṉa.
kumpakōṇattil kuñcitapātam ceṭṭiyār perum celvar. avar ōr aṟanilaiyattait tōṟṟuvittār. ivvāṟē ovvoru periya nakarattilum aṟanilaiyaṅkaḷ ēṟpaṭutta avvavvūr naṉmakkaḷ muyaṟci eṭuttuk koṇṭaṉar. āyiṉum, tam cukapōkaṅ kaḷaik kuṟaittuk koṇṭu ēḻaikaḷ vāḻap poruḷutavi ceyyac celvar palar iṉṉum muṉvaravillai. eṉavē, cila ūrkaḷil maṭṭumē ciṟanta muṟaiyil aṟanilaiyaṅkaḷ tōṉṟalāyiṉa.

inta nilaiyil caṭṭa maṉṟat tērtal neruṅkiyatu. “namatu tiṭṭam nāṭṭil niṟaivēṟṟappaṭa vēṇṭumāyiṉ, nammavar caṭṭa maṉṟattil iṭam peṟutal vēṇṭum. eṉavē, māmā tērtalil niṟpatu nallatu” eṉṟu maṇivaṇṇaṉ kūṟiṉāṉ. “eṉakkup patil maṅkai niṟpatutāṉ nallatu” eṉṟār muttappar.
ivvāṟu ivarkaḷ pēcikkoṇṭirunta atē camayattil aḻakāpuri aṭikaḷiṉ uḷḷattilum itē eṇṇam tōṉṟiyatu. avar avvūrp perumakkaḷ nālvarōṭu vantu maṅkaiyaik kaṇṭār; tām karutiyatait terivittār.
appōtu maṅkai avarai aṉpōṭu nōkki, “aṭikaḷē, ippoḻutu namatu nāṭṭil oruvar tāmē niṉṟu, `nāṉ tērtalukku niṟkiṟēṉ. eṉakku vākkaḷiyuṅkaḷ. nāṉpōy ataic ceykiṟēṉ, itaic ceykiṟēṉ’ eṉṟu kūṟum muṟaiyai nāṉ aṟavē veṟukkiṟēṉ. mekkālē coṉṉatupōla, oru tokuti makkaḷ tammuḷ tāmē kūṭit taṅkaḷ nampikkaikku uriya oruvarait tērtalil niṟutta vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam ovvoru tokutiyilum muṉkūṭṭip potumakkaḷē oruvarait tērnteṭuttu viṭṭāl, vēṟu palarum tērtalil niṉṟu poruḷai iḻakkamāṭṭār. palar poruḷum vīṇākātu. aracāṅkattiṟkum tērtal celavu mikak kuṟaiyum. inta muṟai eṉṟu varumō, aṉṟutāṉ inta nāṭu uruppeṟum.
entak kaṭciyaic cērntiruntālum, makkaḷukkup paṇattaik koṭuttuttāṉ tērtalukku niṟpavar vākkup peṟukiṟār. mikac cila iṭaṅkaḷiltāṉ vākkāḷar, tērtalukku niṟpavariṉ collāṟ ṟalaiyum ceyalāṟṟalaiyum kaṇṭu vākkaḷikkiṉṟaṉar. aṭikaḷē, tiruppōrūriliruntu ceṅkaṟpaṭṭu varaiyilum uḷḷa nilapparappu oru tokuti yākum. it tokutiyil oruvar tērnteṭukkappaṭa vēṇṭum. ittokuti makkaḷ taṅkaḷ cārpil nāṉ uṟuppiṉarākac cellavēṇṭum eṉṟu virumpuvarāyiṉ, nāṉ cellat taṭaiyillai. tāṅkaḷ yōcittu muṭivu kūṟuṅkaḷ” eṉṟāḷ.
nāṭṭil evvāṟu tērtal naṭaipeṟa vēṇṭum eṉpatai avaḷ vāyilākak kēṭṭa aṭikaḷ viyappuṟṟu niṉṟār; avaḷatu āḻnta akaṉṟa araciyal aṟivaip pārāṭṭiṉār. “ammā, eṉ aṟivil ituvaraiyilum inta muṟai taṭṭuppaṭavillai. uṇmaiyil itumātiritāṉ tērtal naṭaipeṟa vēṇṭum. itaṉai ippōtē ceyalil ceyvōm. nāṉē itaṟkuriya ēṟpāṭṭaic ceyvēṉ. ēḻai makkaḷ tuyar nīṅka vēṇṭumāyiṉ nīṅkaḷ caṭṭamaṉṟam cellattāṉ vēṇṭum” eṉṟu kūṟi viṭaipeṟṟār.
avar nērē pāṇṭiyaṉiṭam ceṉṟār; maṅkaiyiṉ karuttai aṟivittār. avaṉaiyum centāmaraiyaiyum aḻaittukkoṇṭu kuḷattūr ceṉṟaṉar; kaṇṇaṉaiyum tēṉmoḻiyaiyum aḻaittuk koṇṭu, at tokuti yiluḷḷa ovvōr ūrukkum ceṉṟaṉar. ovvōr ūrilum potukkūṭṭam kūṭṭiṉar; maṅkaiyait tērnteṭuppatāl varum nalaṉkaḷai eṭuttu viḷakkiṉar.
piṉpu ovvōr ūrārum ūrkkūṭṭam kūṭṭiṉar; naṉku yōcit taṉar; piṟarkkut toṇṭu ceyvataiyē piṟavi eṭuttataṉ nōkka mākak koṇṭa maṅkaiyarkkaraciyaiyē caṭṭamaṉṟa uṟuppiṉarāka niṟkumāṟu vēṇṭikkoṇṭaṉar. ivvāṟu attokuti makkaḷ aṉaivarum muṭivu ceytu vēṇṭukōḷ kaṭitaṅkaḷai maṅkaikku aṉuppiṉar. aṭikaḷ mutaliya potunalat toṇṭarkaḷum palavūrp perumakkaḷum orunāḷ aṟanilaiyattiṟku vantaṉar; maṅkaiyai vaṟpuṟuttic caṭṭamaṉṟattiṟku uṟuppiṉarāka niṟkumāṟu ceytaṉar.
tērtal nāḷ vantatu. ovvoru tokutiyilum palar pōṭṭi yiṭṭaṉar; paṇattai vāri iṟaittaṉar. āṉāl tiruppōrūrt tokutiyil ittakaiya paṇac celavu illai; evarum tērtalil pōṭṭiyiṭavum illai. icceyti ceytittāḷkaḷil veḷiyā yiṟṟu. nāṭṭu naṉmakkaḷ ipputiya muṟaiyai viyantu pārāṭṭiṉar.

makkaḷ nalak kaṭciyiṉarē perumpālarākat tērnteṭukkap peṟṟaṉar. akkaṭciyiṉ talaivarāṉa vaṇaṅkāmuṭi amaiccar avaiyai amaikkat toṭaṅkiṉār. avarum muṉṉāḷ payirttoḻil amaiccar parantāmaṉārum aṟanilaiyattiṟku vantaṉar. maṅkai entak kaṭciyaiyum cērātavaḷāka iruntālum, avaḷ ceytu vanta toṇṭiṉaip pārāṭṭip payirt toḻil tuṟaikku avaḷai amaiccarāka amartta virumpuvatākavum, avaḷ taṉ paṇṇaiyil ceyta cīrtiruttattaiyē caṭṭa vāyilāka nāṭu muḻutum ceyyalāmeṉṟum, makkaḷ nalam oṉṟaiyē karutupavaḷ ātalāl avaḷatu ceyalkaḷil tām talaiyiṭuvatillai eṉṟum vaṇaṅkāmuṭi kūṟi, amaiccar pataviyai ēṟṟukkoḷḷumpaṭi vēṇṭiṉār. muttapparum aḻakāpuri aṭikaḷum maṇivaṇṇaṉ mutaliyōrum avaḷai vēṇṭikkoṇṭaṉar. aṉaivar vēṇṭukōḷmītu avaḷ patavi ēṟka oppukkoṇṭāḷ. 
mutaḷnāḷ naṭaipeṟṟa caṭṭamaṉṟak kūṭṭattil maṅkai eḻuntu niṉṟāḷ. caṭṭamaṉṟa uṟuppiṉar aṉaivarum- etirkkaṭciyiṉar uḷpaṭa- kaitaṭṭi āravāram ceytaṉar. etirkkaṭcit talaivar ēkāmparam eḻuntu maṅkaiyiṉ potunalat toṇṭaip pārāṭṭip pēci avaḷōṭu tam kaṭciyiṉar ottuḻaikkat tayār eṉpatai makiḻcciyōṭu terivittār. vaṇaṅkāmuṭi mutaliya makkaḷ nalakkaṭcit talaivarkaḷum avaḷ varavaip pārāṭṭip pēciṉar; avaḷ tērnteṭukkap peṟṟa putu muṟaiyai mika viyantu pārāṭṭiṉar.
“amaiccar perumakkaḷē, caṭṭa maṉṟa uṟuppiṉarkaḷē, eṉṉaip pārāṭṭi vāḻttuk kūṟiya uṅkaḷ aṉaivarkkum eṉatu paṇivārnta vaṇakkam uriyatākum. eṉ tokuti makkaḷ viruppattiṉpaṭi nāṉ uṟuppiṉaḷ āṉēṉ; mutal amaiccar pōṉṟa perumakkaḷ vaṟpuṟuttalālum, potu makkaḷukku nalam puriyalām eṉṟa nampikkaiyālumē amaiccar pataviyai ēṟṟuk koṇṭē.ṉ. eṅkaḷ paṇṇaiyil nilakkiḻār- paṇṇaiyāṭkaḷ oppantam ceytukoṇṭu irutiṟattārum oṉṟupaṭṭa uḷattarāyp payirt toḻilai naṭattum muṟaiyai nīṅkaḷ naṉku aṟivīrkaḷ. eṅkaḷ muṟaiyaip piṉpaṟṟi vēṟu cila paṇṇaikaḷum iyaṅkivarukiṉṟaṉa. inta muṟaiyaip perumakkaḷākiya nīṅkaḷ ārāyuṅkaḷ; vēṇṭum tiruttaṅkaḷaic ceyyuṅkaḷ.
itaṟkiiṭayil yāṉ ūrtōṟum cuṟṟi, ittiṭṭattiṟkup paṇṇaiyārkaḷiṉ ātaravaiyum paṇṇaiyāṭkaḷiṉ ātaravaiyum tiraṭṭuvēṉ. ivviru tiṟattāriṉ ātaravaiyum peṟṟapiṟakē ittiṭṭam paṟṟiya caṭṭam im maṉṟattil ceyyappeṟum. vāḻap piṟanta makkaḷuṭaiya maṉattaip pakkuvappaṭuttiya piṟakē- avarkaḷ itu nāṭṭukku nallatu eṉpatai uṇarntu icaivu terivitta piṟakē ittiṭṭam caṭṭamātal vēṇṭum eṉpatu eṉatu paṇivāṉa karuttu. tāṉē kaṉiyāta paḻattait taṭikoṇṭu aṭittuk kaṉiyavaikka nāṉ virumpavillai.”
maṅkaiyiṉ poruḷ niṟainta ippēccaik kēṭṭa avaiyōr akam mika makiḻntaṉar. etirkkaṭcit talaivar eḻuntu, “uṅkaḷ karuttai muḻumaṉattuṭaṉ etirkkaṭciyiṉar ātarikkiṉṟaṉar. uṅkaḷ tiṭṭattiṟku eṅkaḷ maṉamārnta vāḻttukkaḷait terivikkiṟōm.” eṉṟu makiḻcciyōṭu kūṟi amarntaṉar.
mutalamaiccar vaṇaṅkāmuṭi eḻuntu, “taṉṉalamaṟṟa amaiccar maṅkaiyarkkaraciyiṉ karuttai aracāṅkam ēṟṟukkoḷkiṟatu. pārāṭṭattakum ivvariya tiṭṭattai nilakkiḻārkaḷ aṉaivarum paranta maṉappāṉmaiyōṭum irakka uṇarcciyōṭum nāṭṭiṉ nalattil karuttuṭaṉum ārāyntu ēṟṟukkoḷḷa virumpukiṟēṉ. ivvariya tiṭṭattaik koṇṭu vantuḷḷa amaiccarkku aracāṅkam taṉ muḻu ātaravaiyum koṭukkiṉṟatu eṉpatai mīṇṭum makiḻcciyōṭu terivittukkoḷkiṟēṉ” eṉṟu akamakiḻcci yuṭaṉ pēci muṭittār.
maṅkaiyarkkaraciyaip pārkkavēṇṭum- avaḷatu pēccaik kēṭkavēṇṭum eṉṟu virumpip pala ūrkaḷiliruntu perumakkaḷ palar caṭṭamaṉṟap pārvaiyāḷarāka vantu iruntaṉar. avarkaḷ aṉaivarum maṅkaiyiṉ eḷiya tūya uṭaikaḷaiyum aṉpu tavaḻum mukattaiyum amaitiyāṉa iṉiya pēccaiyum kaṇṭum kēṭṭum aḷavaṟṟa makiḻcci aṭaintaṉar; taṉakkeṉa vāḻāta ammātaraciyiṉ virinta cintaiyaiyum paranta nōkkattaiyum eṇṇi eṇṇi viyantaṉar.
amaiccar maṅkaiyarkkaraciyiṉ putiya tiṭṭam avaḷatu caṭṭamaṉṟap pēccōṭum paṭattōṭum ellāc ceytit tāḷkaḷilum veḷivantaṉa. ellāk kaṭcic ceytit tāḷkaḷum avaḷatu tiṭṭattaip palapaṭap pārāṭṭiṉa; “nāṭṭukku oru naṅkai”, “mātaruḷ māṇikkam”, “taṉṉalamaṟṟa tūyavaḷ” eṉṟu avaḷukkup paṭṭaṅkoṭuttu makiḻntaṉa.
ovvōr ūrilum potumakkaḷuḷ nāṭṭu nalattilē karuttuṭaiyavar- kaṟṟaṟinta periyōr eṉap palatiṟappaṭṭavar potukkūṭṭaṅkaḷaik kūṭṭiṉar; maṅkaiyiṉ tiṭṭattai viḷakkip pēciṉar; potumakkaḷiṉ ātaravait tēṭiṉar; paṇṇaiyārkaḷiṉ ottuḻaippaiyum vēṇṭiṉar. nāṭeṅkum ‘paṇṇaiyār- paṇṇaiyāḷ’ tiṭṭam paṟṟiyē pēccu naṭantatu. kāntiyaṭikaḷiṉ peyar avaratu taṉṉalamaṟṟa toṇṭiṉāl evvāṟu mūlaimuṭukkukaḷil ellām makkaḷāl uccarikkappaṭṭatō, avvāṟē maṅkaiyark karaciyiṉ peyarum ittiṭṭattāl tamiḻakam muḻutum paraviyatu.

35. ‘maṅkaiyarkkut taṉi araci’
maṅkaiyarkkaraci amaiccar patavi ēṟṟu iraṇṭu āṇṭukaḷ kaḻintaṉa. kaḻinta iraṇṭu āṇṭuk kālattil avaḷ tamiḻakap pērūrkaḷaiyum ciṟṟūrkaḷaiyum pārvai yiṭṭāḷ; āṅkāṅku irunta perunilakkiḻārkaḷaic cantittup pēciṉāḷ. nilat toṭarpāṉa aracāṅka aluvalarkaḷai aṅkaṅkē cantittut taṉ tiṭṭattai makkaḷiṭamum paṇṇaiyārkaḷiṭamum eṭuttu viḷakkumpaṭi vēṇṭikkoṇṭāḷ.
ovvoru māvaṭṭattilum nilakkiḻār kūṭṭam kūṭṭappaṭṭatu. maṅkai ovvoru kūṭṭattilum taṉ tiṭṭattai eṭuttu viḷakkiṉāḷ. pāṇṭiyaṉum nakkīraṉum avaḷ cārpil pala kūṭṭaṅkaḷil pēciṉar. āyirakkaṇakkāṉa āṇṭukaḷākac cuyanalam oṉṟilēyē ūṟi ūṟi vaḷarntu vanta nilakkiḻārkaḷ eḷitil icaivarā? avaruḷ ōraḷavu naṭuvunilai nōkkum paranta maṉappāṉmaiyum koṇṭavar cilarē. avarkaḷ maṉamuvantu avaḷatu tiṭṭattai ēṟṟuk koṇṭaṉar; caṭṭam varuvataṟku muṉṉarē attiṭṭattaic ceyaṟpaṭuttavum muyaṉṟaṉar.
ivvāṟu ovvōr māvaṭṭattilum nūṟṟukku irupattaintu nilakkiḻār vītam maṅkaiyiṉ tiṭṭattaic ceyaṟpaṭuttat toṭaṅkiṉar. eñciya eḻupattaintu pērum paṇṇaiyāṭkaḷai ōraḷavu aṉpāka naṭattat toṭaṅkiṉar; ciṟitaḷavu kūliyum kūṭṭikkoṭuttaṉar. avarkaḷ maṉam inta aḷavukkuttāṉ iṭam koṭuttatu. āyiṉum, amaiccar maṅkaiyarkkaraci maṉam taḷaravillai. avaḷ mukamalarcciyōṭu ap paṇṇaiyārkaḷai aṭikkaṭi pārttu, nalla muṟaiyil avartam uḷḷaṅkaḷai māṟṟa muyaṉṟāḷ. mutal amaiccar uḷḷiṭṭa piṟa amaiccarkaḷum etirkkaṭciyiṉarum tattam pakutiyil it tiṭṭattiṟku ātaravu tēṭiṉar. ivvāṟu amaiccarkaḷum caṭṭamaṉṟa uṟuppiṉarum eṭuttukkoṇṭa iṭaiviṭā muyaṟciyāl nūṟṟukku muppatupēr ōrāṇṭiṟkuḷ maṅkaiyiṉ tiṭṭattai ēṟṟuk koṇṭaṉar.
iṅṅaṉam paṭippaṭiyāka maṅkaiyiṉ tiṭṭam nāṭṭil ceyaṟ paṭuttappaṭṭatu. nāṉkām āṇṭil maṅkai miṉṉal vēkattil tamiḻakam muḻutum cuṟṟiṉāḷ. muttappar, aḻakāpuri aṭikaḷ, maṇi vaṇṇaṉ, nakkīraṉ, pāṇṭiyaṉ, kaṇṇaṉ mutaliya āṭavarum, centāmarai, tēṉmoḻi, tāmaraik kaṇṇi mutaliya peṇmaṇikaḷum pala ūrkaḷukkum ceṉṟu maṅkaiyiṉ tiṭṭattiṟku ātaravu tēṭiṉar. avarkaḷ ceṉṟa iṭantōṟum potumakkaḷ ātaravu mikutiyākak kiṭaittatu.
“it tiṭṭattiṟku ātaravu taravillaiyāyiṉ, paṇṇaiyil vēlai ceyyamāṭṭōm” eṉṟu pala ūrkaḷil paṇṇaiyāṭkaḷ kiḷarcci ceyyalāyiṉar. maṅkai avviṭaṅkaḷukku viraintāḷ; “paṇṇaic cakōtararkaḷē, nīṅkaḷ ituvaraiyil paṇṇaiyil vēlai ceytu vantatataippōlavē iṉiyum vēlai ceytu varavēṇṭum. nīṅkaḷ vēlai ceyya maṟuttāl, nāṭṭu viḷaiccal kuṟaiyum. ataṉāl paṇṇaiyār tuṉpappaṭamāṭṭār; uṇavu kiṭaikkāmal potu makkaḷtām tuṉpappaṭuvar; nīṅkaḷum tuṉpappaṭa nēriṭum. uṅkaḷ kaṭamaiyai nīṅkaḷ ceytu koṇṭu iruṅkaḷ. ittiṭṭattiṟku iṇaṅkumpaṭi paṇṇaiyārai vēṇṭikkoḷḷuvatu eṅkaḷ kaṭamai. eṉ collai mīṟi nīṅkaḷ vēlai niṟuttam ceytāl, nāṉ amaiccar pataviyiliruntu vilakiviṭuvēṉ. nāṉ uṅkaḷukkākavē ippataviyil irukkiṟēṉ eṉpatai maṟantuviṭātīrkaḷ.”
paṇṇaiyāṭkaḷ avaḷatu aṟivuraiyai ēṟṟuk koṇṭaṉar; vaḻakkampōl paṇṇai vēlaikaḷaic ceyyalāyiṉar.
kiḷarcci ceytu vēlai niṟuttam ceyyat toṭaṅkiya paṇṇaiyāṭkaḷ maṅkaiyarkkaraciyiṉ aṟivuraiyaik kēṭṭup peṭṭippāmpukaḷ pōl aṭaṅki oḻuṅkāka vēlai ceyyat toṭaṅkiyataik kaṇṭa paṇṇaiyār cilar maṉam makiḻntaṉar; nallaṟivu peṟṟaṉar; putiya tiṭṭattai ēṟṟukkoṇṭaṉar.
iṅṅaṉam maṅkaiyiṉ iṭaiviṭā uḻaippāl avaḷ patavi ēṟṟa nāṉkām āṇṭiṉ iṟutikkuḷ nūṟṟukku eḻupattaintu paṇṇaiyār putiya tiṭṭattaic ceyal paṭuttalāyiṉar. eñciya irupattaintu pēr viṭākkaṇṭarāka iruntaṉar. āyiṉum, karumattilēyē kaṇṇāyirunta maṅkaiyarkkaraci vāḷā irukkavillai; muttappar, aṭikaḷ, maṇivaṇṇaṉ, nakkīraṉ ākiya nālvaraiyum appaṇṇai yārkaḷiṉ maṉappōkkai māṟṟa aṉuppiṉāḷ.
aracāṅka nilavari atikārikaḷuḷ perumpālōr nilakkiḻār allar. eṉavē, avar aṉaivarum maṅkaiyiṉ tiṭṭattai maṉamāra ātarittaṉar; taṅkaḷ taṅkaḷ vaṭṭaṅkaḷilum māvaṭṭaṅ kaḷilum irunta paṇṇaiyār kaḷuṭaṉ pēci, avartam uḷḷaṅ kaḷai māṟṟa muyaṉṟaṉar; ēḻaikaḷiṉ pārāṭṭutalukku uriya varākumpaṭi aṟivuruttiṉar. ittakaiya palamāṉa muyaṟciyiṉāl mēlum cilar attiṭṭattaic ceyaṟpaṭutta muṉaintaṉar.
nāṭu muḻuvatilum kaṇakkiṭṭup pārttatil, nūṟṟukkut toṇṇūṟu nilakkiḻār vītam putiya tiṭṭattaic ceyaṟpaṭuttat toṭaṅkiṉar eṉṟa uṇmai terintatu. itaṟkuḷ maṅkai pataviyēṟṟu nāṉku āṇṭukaḷum oṉpatu mātaṅkaḷum muṭintuviṭṭaṉa. nūṟṟukkup pattuppērē nāṭu veṟukkum paṇpaṟṟavarāka iruntaṉar.
appattuppēraiyum icaivitta piṟakē tiṭṭam paṟṟiya caṭṭattai niṟaivēṟṟa vēṇṭum eṉṟu maṅkai eṇṇiṉāḷ. “alai ōynta piṟaku kaṭalil nīrāṭavēṇṭum’ eṉṟu niṉaittāl- alai ōyappōvatum illai; nām nīrāṭappōvatum illai. eñciya pattuppērum oppuk koṇṭa piṟakē caṭṭam ceyyavēṇṭum eṉṟu niṉaittāl avarkaḷ icaiya mēlum pala varuṭaṅkaḷākalām. iṉṉum mūṉṟu mātaṅkaḷil putiya tērtal varukiṉṟatu. ātalāl, ataṟkuḷ itaṉaic caṭṭamākkuvatu nallatu. ipputiya tiṭṭam caṭṭamākkappaṭiṉ, aṭutta muṟaiyum nāmē aracāṅkattai iyakka nērum. appoḻutu mēlum pala nalla tiṭṭaṅkaḷai uruvākkalām” eṉṟu mutalamaiccar moḻintār.
maṅkai avaratu yōcaṉaiyai ēṟṟukkoṇṭāḷ; etirkkaṭcit talaivaraic cantittu avar yōcaṉaiyaik kēṭṭāḷ. mutalamaiccar kūṟiyataiyē avarum kūṟiṉār. putiya tiṭṭam caṭṭacapaiyil paṭikkappaṭṭuc caṭṭamākkappaṭum eṉṟa ceyti aracāṅka aṟikkaiyilum ceytittāḷkaḷilum veḷiyiṭappaṭṭatu. potumakkaḷ aḷavaṟṟa makiḻcci aṭaintaṉar.
caṭṭacapaiyil putiya tiṭṭam maṅkaiyarkkaraciyāl paṭittu viḷakkappaṭṭatu. pala kaṭci uṟuppiṉarum pala aiyaṅkaḷaik kēṭṭu teḷintaṉar. piṉṉar ellā uṟuppiṉar opputal mītu attiṭṭam caṭṭamākkappaṭṭatu. mutal amaiccarum etirkaṭcit talaivarum amaiccar maṅkaiyark karaciyaiyum avaḷ koṇṭuvanta tiṭṭattaiyum mikavum pārāṭṭip pēciṉar.
“amaiccar perumakkaḷē, etirkaṭcit talaivarē, maṉṟa uṟuppiṉarkaḷē, nāṉ oru tiṭṭattaik koṇṭu vantēṉ. āṉāl ataṟkuc caṭṭamaṉṟattilum veḷi ulakilum muḻu ātaravaiyum koṭutta perumakkaḷ nīṅkaḷē. uṅkaḷ ottuḻaippu illai yāyiṉum nilakkiḻārkaḷiṉ ottuḻaippu illaiyāyiṉum ic caṭṭam niṟaivēṟātu. ākavē, paṇṇaiyāḷkaḷiṉ nalattai virumpum it tiṭṭattaic caṭṭamākkiya perumaiyum naṭai muṟaiyil koṇarnta perumaiyum nilakkiḻārkaḷaiyum uṅkaḷaiyumē cārum. uṅkaḷ aṉaivarukkum .iṟaivaṉ tiruvaruḷ purivāṉāka! eṉ maṉamārnta naṉṟiyaiyum vaṇakkattaiyum uṅkaḷukkut terivittuk koḷḷukiṟēṉ” eṉṟu kūṟik kaikkūppi vaṇaṅkiṉāḷ.
avaḷ pēccu muṭintatum caṭṭa maṉṟa uṟuppiṉarum pārvaiyāḷarum neṭunēram kaitaṭṭi āravāram ceytaṉar. maṅkaiyarkkaraci eḻuntu mīṇṭum kaikūppi vaṇaṅkiṉāḷ. appoḻutu ‘ṭūmīl’ eṉṟu tuppākki veṭiyōcai kēṭṭatu. tuppākiyiṉiṉṟum veḷippaṭṭa kuṇṭu maṅkaiyarkkaraciyiṉ mārpait tuḷaittatu. avaḷ kūppiya kaikaḷuṭaṉ nāṟkāliyiṉ mītu cāyntāḷ.
caṭṭamaṉṟam paraparappaṭaintatu. caṭṭa maṉṟak katavukaḷ uṭaṉē tāḷiṭappaṭṭaṉa. ovvoru vāyililum pōlīcār kāval vaikkappaṭṭaṉar. pārvaiyāḷaruḷ oruvaṉāka iruntu cuṭṭavaṉ piṭipaṭṭāṉ. pōlīcār uṭaṉē avaṉait taṉicciṟaiyil aṭaittu vaittaṉar.
amaiccarkaḷum caṭṭamaṉṟa uṟuppiṉarum aṭainta tuyarattiṟku aḷavillai. maṅkaiyiṉ uṭal avaḷ vaḷamaṉaikku eṭuttuc cellappaṭṭatu. aṅku ellōrum kūṭiviṭṭaṉar. nāṭṭukku nalam viḷaitta naṅkai oruvaṉāl cuṭṭuk kollappaṭṭāḷ. eṉṟa ceyti kāṭṭuttīppōl nakaram muḻutum paraviyatu. veḷiyūrkaḷukkut tantikaḷ paṟantaṉa. ‘avaḷ uṭalai maṟunāḷ aṭakkam ceyyuṅkaḷ. nāṅkaḷ vantuviṭuvōm’ eṉṟu pala tantikaḷ mutal amaiccarukku vantaṉa. maṅkaiyiṉ kaḻuttil mālai iṭampeṟṟatu. avaḷatu āviyaṟṟa uṭal vaḷamaṉaiyiṉ periya kūṭattil, varupavar eḷitil pārkkumpaṭi vaikkappaṭṭiruntatu. ilaṭcakkaṇak kāṉa ceṉṉai nakaramakkaḷ vantu pārttu, avaḷ tiruvaṭikaḷait toṭṭuk kaṇkaḷil oṟṟikkoṇṭu ceṉṟaṉar.
maṟunāṭ kālai avvaḷamaṉaiyiṉ paranta veḷi yiṭattil mēṭai iṭampeṟṟatu. ammēṭaiyil avaḷatu uṭal kiṭattap peṟṟatu. veḷiyūrkaḷiliruntu vanta perumakkaḷ ovvoruvarākac ceṉṟu avaḷukkut taṅkaḷ iṟuti vaṇakkattait terivittuk koṇṭaṉar. innikaḻccikkup piṟaku iraṅkal kūṭṭam naṭaipeṟṟatu. mutal amaiccar vaṇaṅkāmuṭi kūṭṭattiṟkut talaimai tāṅkiṉār.
avar eḻuntu niṉṟār; kaṇṇīr viṭṭuk kataṟiṉār. avarāl pēcavē muṭiyavillai. etirkkaṭcit talaivar aḻutukoṇṭē avaḷatu peruntoṇṭai naṉku viḷakkiyum pārāṭṭiyum pēciṉār. piṉpu ovvoru māvaṭṭattiṟkum oruvar vītam- avaḷ toṇṭu kaḷaiyum paṇpukaḷaiyum pārāṭṭip pēciṉar.
avaḷatu uṭal tiruppōrūr aṟanilaiyattil aṭakkam ceyyappaṭum eṉpatu aṟivikkappaṭṭatu. aṉṟu mālai tiruppōrūril eḷ iṭa iṭamillai. pallāyirakkaṇakkil makkaḷ kūṭiviṭṭaṉar. vaḻipāṭṭu maṇṭapattiṟku etiril irunta ciṟiya pūṅkāviṉ naṭuvil maṅkaiyiṉ uṭal aṭakkam ceyyappaṭṭatu. avvamayam, īṉṟa tāyai iḻanta kuḻantaikaḷ pōla amaiccar mutal āṇṭi īṟāka irunta aṉaivarum kaṇṇīr viṭṭuk kataṟiṉar. appoḻutu muttappar ellōraiyum kaikūppi vaṇaṅkiṉār; “perumakkaḷē, eṉṉaip potu makkaḷ nalattiṟkāka uḻaikkumpaṭi tūṇṭiṉavaḷ maṅkaiyarkkaracitāṉ. ulakattār pārvaiyil avaḷ eṉakku makaḷē tavira, uṇmaiyil eṉṉaiyum ēḻaikaḷaiyum vāḻvikka vanta teyvam eṉṟē nāṉ avaḷai matikkiṉṟēṉ. avaḷ kollappaṭṭatu paṟṟi nāṉ varuntavillai. avaḷ virumpiya tiṭṭattai niṟaivēṟṟiya piṟaku kollappaṭṭāḷ eṉpatu eṉakku āṟutalait tarukiṉṟatu. amerikkāvil āprakām liṅkaṉ aṭimai nīkkac caṭṭam niṟaivēṟṟiya piṟakutāṉ oru nāṭaka maṉṟattil cuṭṭuk kollappaṭṭār. āṉāl maṅkaiyarkkaraciyō, caṭṭam niṟaivēṟiyavuṭaṉ- perumakkaḷ pārāṭṭutalukkiṭaiyē- caṭṭa maṉṟattilēyē cuṭṭuk kollappaṭṭāḷ.
avaḷaic cuṭṭuk koṉṟavar, avaḷatu tiṭṭattai itukāṟum niṟaivēṟṟa maṟutta oru paṇṇaiyār eṉṟu pōlīs kūṟukiṟatu. avaḷ taṉ tiṭṭattiṟkākat taṉ uyiraip pali koṭuttāḷ. iṉiyāvatu eñciya paṇṇaiyārkaḷ caṭṭamāṉa tiṭṭattaic ceyaṟpaṭuttumpaṭi vēṇṭik koḷḷukiṟēṉ. maṅkaiyarkkaraciyiṉ uyarnta karuttukkaḷ nāṭṭil paravavēṇṭum- camutāyattiṟku nalam viḷaivikka vēṇṭum- nāṭṭil vaṟumai nīṅka vēṇṭum eṉpatuvē eṉ āval. nām aṉaivarum ittuṟaiyil muyaṉṟu iṭaiviṭātu pāṭupaṭutal oṉṟē, avaḷatu āṉmāvukku amaitiyait tarum” eṉṟu amaitiyākap pēciṉār.
mutal amaiccar vaṇaṅkāmuṭi, “maṅkaiyarkkaraci vāḻka! avaratu aṟacceyal vāḻka!” eṉṟu kūṟiṉār. ellōrum avar coṉṉapaṭiyē urattuk kūṟi vāḻttiṉar.
aḻakāpuri aṭikaḷ, “periyōrkaḷē, ēṟattāḻa āyirattu muṉṉūṟu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ maturaiyil pāṇṭiyaṉ maṉaiviyāka oru maṅkaiyarkkaraci tōṉṟiṉāḷ. avaḷ muyaṟciyāl pāṇṭiya nāṭṭil caivam taḻaittatu. namatu maṅkaiyarkkaraciyāl piccaikkārar kuṭimakkaḷāka māṟiṉar; paṇṇaiyāṭkaḷ vāḻkkaiyil nalam peṟṟaṉar; anātaip piḷḷaikaḷ kuṭimakkaḷāyiṉar; anātaip peṇkaḷum kaimpeṇkaḷum māṉattōṭu piḻaikkum vaḻi kaṟṟuk koṇṭaṉar. anta maṅkaiyarkkaraciyaip periya purāṇam pāṭiya cēkkiḻār perumāṉ, `maṅkaiyarkkut taṉiyaraci- eṅkaḷ teyvam’ eṉṟu vāyāra vāḻttiṉār. ceyaṟkariya ceyta namatu perumāṭṭiyaiyum nām `maṅkaiyarkkut taṉiyaraci- –eṅkaḷ teyvam’ eṉṟu vāyāra vāḻttuvōmāka! appeṇṇaraci viṭṭuc ceṉṟa aṟappaṇikaḷai nām muḻu maṉattuṭaṉ ceytu veṟṟi kāṇpōmāka!” eṉṟu pēcik kaikuvittu vaṇaṅkiṉār.
uṭaṉē kūṭṭattiṉar, “maṅkaiyarkkut taṉiyaraci vāḻka”, “eṅkaḷ teyvam vāḻka!” eṉṟu vāḻttoliyai eḻuppiṉar.

mūṉṟu mātaṅkaḷ kaṭantaṉa. caṭṭamaṉṟattil uḷḷa uyarnta mēṭaiyil maṅkaiyarkkaraciyiṉ uruvaccilai iṭam peṟṟatu. atupōṉṟa aḻakiya uruvaccilai avaḷatu uṭalaip putaitta iṭattilum niṟuttappeṟṟatu: vaḻipāṭṭu maṇṭapattilum oṉṟu iṭam peṟṟatu; perumpālāṉa paṇṇaiyār māḷikaikaḷilum avaḷ uruvappaṭaṅkaḷ iṭampeṟṟaṉa. ivai aṉaittiṟkum mēlāka, ēḻaimakkaḷ uḷḷaṅkaḷil avaḷ niṉaivu iṭampeṟṟatu.
aṭutta tērtalil maṇivaṇṇaṉ makkaḷāl tērnteṭukkappaṭṭāṉ. vaṇaṅkāmuṭi mīṇṭum mutal amaiccar āṉār. maṅkai tāṅkiya amaiccar patavi maṇivaṇṇaṉukku vaḻaṅkappeṟṟatu.

maṅkai cuṭṭukkollappaṭṭatu koṭiyavar maṉattaiyum kuluṅkac ceytatu. avaḷaic cuṭṭuk koṉṟa paṇṇaiyār oru kuṭikāraṉ- kūttikkaḷḷaṉ- koṭiyavaṉ eṉṟu ūr makkaḷāl veṟukkappaṭṭavaṉ; putiya tiṭṭam ceyaṟpaṭumāyiṉ tāṉ atukāṟum nukarntu vanta cukapōkaṅkaḷ keṭṭuviṭumē eṉṟa kavalaiyāl avaḷaic cuṭṭukkollat tuṇintāṉ. caṭ.ṭam niṟaivēṟṟappaṭuvataṟku muṉṉarē caṭṭamaṉṟattil avaḷaik kolla niṉaintu avaṉ pārvaiyār iṭattil amarntiruntāṉ. nāṭṭiṉ nallakālam avaṉ cuṭuvataṟku muṉ caṭṭam niṟaivēṟiviṭṭatu. avaṉ taṉ kuṟṟattai oppukkoṇṭāṉ. avaṉukkut tūkkut taṇṭaṉai vitikkappaṭṭatu.
atuvaraiyil maṅkaiyiṉ tiṭṭattai ēṟkātirunta piṟar avaḷ iṟappukkup piṉ maṉamāṟṟam peṟṟaṉar; tāṅkaḷ attiṭṭattai ēṟṟukkoṇṭatāka mutal amaiccarukku aṟivittaṉar.

maṇivaṇṇaṉ amaiccaṉākip pēca eḻunta mutal caṭṭa maṉṟak kūṭṭattilēyē itaṉai aṟivittāṉ: “periyōrkaḷē, eṉ āruyir maṉaivi maṅkaiyarkkaraciyiṉ tiṭṭam caṭṭamāṉatu maṭṭumaṉṟu, iṉṟu ellōrālum ceyaṟpaṭuttappaṭṭu varukiṉṟatu eṉpatai makiḻcciyōṭu terivittukkoḷḷukiṟēṉ. itē tiṭṭattaip piṉpaṟṟi iṉi mutalāḷi- toḻilāḷiyiṉ tiṭṭattait tayārikka vēṇṭum. attiṭṭattai etirkkaṭcit talaivarōṭu kalantu uruvākki āṟu mātaṅkaḷuḷ uṅkaḷ muṉpu vaippēṉ. ataṟku uṅkaḷ ātaravu tēvai” eṉṟu kūṟik kaikkuvittu vaṇaṅkiṉāṉ.
avaṉatu putiya tiṭṭattaip pārāṭṭuvataṟku aṟikuṟiyāka uṟuppiṉar aṉaivarum caṭṭamaṉṟamē atirumpaṭi kaitaṭṭi āravārittaṉar.
maṉitap paṇpu vaḷarka!
